`final_report.py`读取并解析完整`outputs/core_manifest.json`对象，写`outputs/final_report.json`，恰有title、component_count、sha256、attention四个字段。title准确为`数仓切换保障`；component_count为core_object[\"components\"]的键数。\n81: sha256的输入对象是完整core_object，包含外层components键；不是仅core_object[\"components\"]、不是原文件字节、不是多个文件摘要拼接。序列化准确为`json.dumps(core_object, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), allow_nan=False).encode(\"utf-8\")`，不加文件末尾换行，然后求SHA-256小写hex。\n82: attention是组件名去重排序列表：对每个result，blocked为真、feasible或compatible显式为false、deny>0、score_total>=20中任一条件成立即列入；不存在的字段不触发，不能把空列表、空对象或数值0等同于显式false。每个组件至多列一次。\n83: \n84: ## 验收与执行边界\n85: \n86: 在工作区运行`python3 输入对象仅当键集合恰好是`{\"name\",\"result\"}`时视为上游组件封装；先读取其result，再使用对应operation约定的字段。原始本地对象不是文件路径，也不当成records或nodes列表。多输入operation必须消费全部指定输入，不能复制固定上游结果。\n- `render.build_component(root: Path, contract_path: Path)`：读取该契约及其全部输入，调用transform并返回完整封装；执行组件render.py时写入contract.output。文件路径相对于工作区root；从任意当前目录启动均应正确，缺文件或子命令失败须失败。\n- int字段严格排除bool；有限数字允许int/float但排除bool、NaN、Infinity。非空文本先检查去首尾空白后不为空；是否返回去空白/小写形式由各operation明确规定，不全局改写身份字段。列表与字典按规定类型读取，不把null当空集合，不自动把数字字符串转换成数字。\n- 证据公共字段\n\n证据公共字段：每条输入均含id:非空str、revision:int>=0、owner:非空str、category:prepare/copy/verify、tags:list[非空str]、failures:int>=0、total:int>=1、lag:int>=0、valid:bool；failures<=total。所有整数排除bool。id去首尾空白；owner/category/tags去空白小写；tags去重排序。先校验全部记录与修订冲突，再取最新版本。只有本operation段落指定的公式决定score，其他operation的公式不适用。\n\n## 各组件的完整业务规则\n\n### domains/backfill — 修订证据失败率\n\noperation：`failure_evidence`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入为记录数组。每项必须有 id:非空字符串、revision:整数>=0、owner:非空字符串、category:prepare/copy/verify、tags:字符串数组、failures:整数>=0、total:整数>=1、lag:整数>=0、valid:布尔；failures<=total。id去首尾空白；owner/category/tags去首尾空白并小写；tags去重排序。先校验每个输入版本，再按规范化id选最大revision。同id同revision的全部规范化字段必须相同，否则ValueError；相同副本去重，即使该版本不是最新版也检查冲突。评分仅为 ceil(20*failures/total)+offset，再截断到[0,20]。不使用lag、sla或valid计算本operation的评分。输出result={records:[{id,source,owner,tags,category,score}],count,score_total}；source=contract.name，records按(source,id)升序，count为最新记录数，score_total为这些记录分数之和。threshold不筛除证据记录。空输入records=[]，count=score_total=0。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 domains/backfill/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### domains/freshness — 修订证据延迟\n\noperation：`lag_evidence`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n记录字段、规范化、全部修订的冲突检查、最新版选择和输出字段与本说明中的证据公共规则一致：id去空白；owner/category/tags去空白小写，tags去重排序；所有版本均校验，按id保留最大revision；相同id/revision规范化内容不一致报ValueError。每条记录仍必须包含failures、total、lag、valid等全部证据字段且满足类型/范围约束。评分仅为ceil(lag/sla)+offset，再截断到[0,20]，sla为契约中的正整数。不把失败率公式叠加到延迟公式上，不用threshold筛除证据记录。result={records:[{id,source,owner,tags,category,score}],count,score_total}，source=contract.name，records按(source,id)排序。空输入为空records及两个零统计。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 domains/freshness/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### domains/checksums — 修订证据完整性\n\noperation：`integrity_evidence`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n记录字段、规范化、全部修订的冲突检查、最新版选择和输出字段与本说明中的证据公共规则一致：id去空白；owner/category/tags去空白小写，tags去重排序；所有版本均校验，按id保留最大revision；相同id/revision规范化内容不一致报ValueError。每条记录仍必须包含failures、total、lag、valid等全部证据字段且满足类型/范围约束。评分仅为(0 if valid else 15)+failures+offset，再截断到[0,20]；不使用ceil(20*failures/total)或延迟公式。不用threshold筛除证据记录。result={records:[{id,source,owner,tags,category,score}],count,score_total}，source=contract.name，records按(source,id)排序。空输入为空records及两个零统计。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 domains/checksums/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### views/cutover_risk — 风险排名\n\noperation：`risk_ranking`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入为按契约inputs顺序传入的组件封装；对每个封装读取result.records。以(source,id)合并：键相同且整条记录相同才可去重，否则ValueError。设合并集合为R，先选score>=threshold，再按(-score,source,id)排序，最后截取前limit项得到S。result.records、ranking、score_total全部基于同一个S，不能让records保留整个R。records仅改变展示顺序，按(source,id)升序；ranking按上述分数排名输出完整source+\":\"+id；score_total=sum(S.score)，不对R求和。输出result恰为{records,ranking,score_total}；S为空时分别为[]、[]、0。不得丢弃source前缀或再次按id跨source合并。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 views/cutover_risk/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### views/reviewer_load — 负载分配\n\noperation：`review_balance`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n合并每个输入封装的result.records，以(source,id)去重，相同键内容冲突ValueError。使用全部合并记录，不再应用threshold、limit或其他筛选。按(-score,source,id)逐条处理，每次选当前累计score最小的reviewer，平局取reviewer名称字典序最小者；加入该记录score。reviewers来自本组件契约，不能只保留最终被分配到任务的人。result={records,assignments,loads}；records按(source,id)；assignments是{id:完整source+\":\"+id,reviewer}并按id排序；loads包含所有reviewer（包括零负载）。空输入仍返回完整零loads，其余两个列表为空。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 views/reviewer_load/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### views/repair_waves — 依赖波次与条件修复\n\noperation：`remediation_plan`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入是组件封装，不是原始nodes数组。合并所有输入的result.records，包括审核分配组件返回的records；以(source,id)去重，相同键整条记录冲突ValueError。只读取records，不使用assignments、loads或feasible推导人员边、job/worker匹配或额外阻断。only_if_blocking=false时保留全部合并记录；为true时，若存在至少一条tags含blocking且score>=threshold的记录，也保留全部记录；否则使用空记录集合。此开关不是逐条筛选器。对保留集合，每条记录对应一个业务节点，完整id为source+\":\"+id，duration=1+score//5；category次序prepare<copy<verify，每个节点依赖集合中所有严格较早类别节点。按下述业务图规则计算waves/earliest_finish/critical_path/makespan：每波取本波开始时所有依赖已提交的节点并按id排序，整波结束后才取下一波；finish=duration+最大前驱finish，无前驱为duration；最大finish并列时，先比较前驱已经形成的完整路径列表（尚未追加当前节点）并取字典序最小，再追加当前节点；最终终点按finish最大、完整路径字典序最小选择。result={records,blocked:false,waves,critical_path,makespan,earliest_finish}；records按(source,id)，earliest_finish键按id。空集合时records/waves/critical_path=[]、makespan=0、earliest_finish={}、blocked仍为false。该业务图规则不表示Agent应怎样拆分或调度任务。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 views/repair_waves/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n## JSON产物、集成与最终报告\n\n所有写出的JSON文件采用UTF-8、ensure_ascii=False、sort_keys=True、indent=2、allow_nan=False，末尾加一个真实换行字符。紧凑摘要序列化与文件展示格式不同。\n`run_all.py`定位工作区，依据真实文件接口完成全部必要计算，执行已有集成与最终报告。不能用文件名排序代替执行条件，不能依赖旧outputs；各子进程失败传播非零退出码。已有`python3 ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), allow_nan=False).encode(\"utf-8\")`，不加文件末尾换行，然后求SHA-256小写hex。\nattention是组件名去重排序列表：对每个result，blocked为真、feasible或compatible显式为false、deny>0、score_total>=20中任一条件成立即列入；不存在的字段不触发，不能把空列表、空对象或数值0等同于显式false。每个组件至多列一次。\n\n## So compare full normalized dict.\n\nLet me store normalized records as full dicts and compare.\n\nAlgorithm:\n```python\ndef _normalize_record(r):\n    ... validate, return norm dict ...\n\ndef transform(payloads, contract):\n    # payloads is list with one element (list of records)\n    # For domain, payloads[0] is the records array (list).\n    records = payloads[0]\n    if not isinstance(records, list):\n        raise ValueError(...)\n    normalized = [_normalize_record(r) for r in records]\n    # group by (id, revision)\n    from collections import defaultdict\n    groups = {}\n    for nr in normalized:\n        key = (nr['id'], nr['revision'])\n        if key in groups:\n            if groups[key] != nr:\n                raise ValueError('conflict')\n        else:\n            groups[key] = nr\n    # select latest per id\n    latest = {}\n    for (rid, rev), nr in groups.items():\n        if rid not in latest or rev > latest[rid]['revision']:\n            latest[rid] = nr\n    # score\n    out_records = []\n    for rid in sorted(latest):  # or sort later\n        nr = latest[rid]\n        score = _score(nr, contract)\n        out_records.append({\n            'id': rid,\n            'source': contract['name'],\n            'owner': nr['owner'],\n            'tags': nr['tags'],\n            'category': nr['category'],\n            'score': score,\n        })\n    out_records.sort(key=lambda r: (r['source'], r['id']))\n    score_total = sum(r['score'] for r in out_records)\n    return {'name': contract['name'], 'result': {\n        'records': out_records,\n        'count': len(out_records),\n        'score_total': score_total,\n    }}\n```\n\nWait, the group check with `groups[key] != nr` — but if there are duplicate identical records, `groups[key]` is already set and equal, so no error, dedup naturally. dict):\n        raise ValueError('record must be object')\n    # id\n    if not isinstance(r.get('id'), str):\n        raise ValueError('id must be string')\n    rid = r['id'].strip()\n    if not    raise ValueError('category invalid')\n    # tags\n    tags = r.get('tags')\n    if not isinstance(tags, list):\n        raise ValueError('tags must be list')\n    norm_tags = []\n    for t in norm_tags.append(tt)\n    norm_tags = sorted(set(norm_tags))\n    # failures\n    f = r.get('failures')\n    if not _is_int(f) or f < 0:\n        raise ValueError('failures invalid')\n    total = r.get('total')\n    if not _is_int(total) or total < 1:\n        raise ValueError('total invalid')\n    lag = r.get('lag')\n    if not _is_int(lag) or lag < 0:\n        raise ValueError('lag invalid')\n    valid = r.get('valid')\n    if not isinstance(valid, bool):\n        raise ValueError('valid must be bool')\n    if f > total:\n        raise ValueError('failures > total')\n    return      'valid': valid,\n    }\n```\n\nThen transform:\n\n```python\ndef _score(n, contract):\n    offset = contract['offset']\n    raw = (20 * n['failures'] + n['total'] - 1) // n['total'] + offset\n    return max(0, min(20, raw))\n\n\ndef transform(payloads, contract):\n    if len(payloads) != 1:\n        raise ValueError('expected one payload')\n    records = payloads[0]\n    if not isinstance(records, list):\n        raise ValueError('payload must be array')\n    norm = [_validate_and_normalize(r) for r in records]\n    groups = {}\n    for nr in norm:\n        key = (nr['id'], nr['revision'])\n        if key in groups:\n            if groups[key] != nr:\n                raise ValueError('revision conflict')\n        else:\n            groups[key] = nr\n    latest = {}\n out.append({\n            'id': rid,\n            'source': contract['name'],\n            'owner': nr['owner'],\n            'tags': nr['tags'],\n            'category': nr['category'],\n dependencies\n    for nid, n in nodes.items():\n        n['deps'] = [oid for oid, o in nodes.items() if cat_order[o['category']] < cat_order[n['category']]]\n    # waves (topological by readiness)\n    waves = []\n    remaining = set(nodes.keys())\n    while remaining:\n        ready = [nid for nid in remaining if all(d not in remaining for d in nodes[nid]['deps'])]\n        if not ready:\n            raise ValueError('cycle')\n        ready.sort()\n        waves.append(ready)\n        for nid in ready:\n            remaining.discard(nid)\n    # finish & path\n    for wave in waves:\n        for nid in wave:\n            n = nodes[nid]\n            deps = n['deps']\n            if not deps:\n                n['finish'] = n['duration']\n                n['path'] = [nid]\n            else:\n                max_f = max(nodes[d]['finish'] for d in deps)\n                candidates = [d for d in deps if nodes[d]['finish'] == max_f]\n                chosen = min(candidates, key=lambda d: nodes[d]['path'])\n                n['finish'] = n['duration'] + max_f\n                n['path'] = nodes[chosen]['path'] + [nid]\n    # endpoint\n    max_finish = max(n['finish'] for n in nodes.values())\n    endpoint_candidates = [nid for nid in nodes if nodes[nid]['finish'] == max_finish]\n    endpoint = min(endpoint_candidates, key=lambda nid: nodes[nid]['path'])\n    records_out = sorted(kept, key=lambda r: (r['source'], r['id']))\n    earliest_finish = {nid: nodes[nid]['finish'] for nid in nodes}\n    return {'name': contract['name'], 'result': {\n        'records': records_out,\n        'blocked': False,\n        'waves': waves,\n        'critical_path': nodes[endpoint]['path'],\n        'makespan': nodes[endpoint]['finish'],\n        'earliest_finish': earliest_finish,\n    }}\n```\n\nWait, careful with `waves`: waves should be a list of lists of node ids, but should empty categories be included? Build adjacency: for each contract, its dependencies = set of component names whose output appears in its inputs.\n\n```python\nimport json, subprocess, sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\n\n\ndef _load_contracts():\n    out = []\n    for p in sorted(ROOT.rglob('contract.json')):\n        rel = p.relative_to(ROOT)\n        if 'outputs' in rel.parts:\n            continue\n        out.append(p)\n    return out\n\n\ndef main():\n    contracts = _load_contracts()\n    info = {}\n    for p in contracts:\n        c = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\n        info[c['name']] = {'path': p, 'contract': c}\n    output_to_name = {c['output']: c['name'] for c in [info[n]['contract'] for n in info]}\n    # build deps\n    for name in info:\n        c = info[name]['contract']\n        info[name]['deps'] = [output_to_name[inp] for inp in c['inputs'] if inp in output_to_name]\n    # Kahn\n    order = []\n    remaining = set(info.keys())\n    while remaining:\n        ready = sorted(n for n in remaining if all(d not in remaining for d in info[n]['deps']))\n        if not ready:\n            raise RuntimeError('dependency cycle')\n        for n in ready:\n            order.append(n)\n            remaining.discard(n)\n    for name in order:\n        p = info[name]['path']\n        subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(p.with_name('render.py'))], cwd=ROOT, check=True, timeout=60)\n    subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / 'integrate.py')], cwd=ROOT, check=True, timeout=60)\n    subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / 'final_report.py')], cwd=ROOT, check=True, timeout=60)\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n```\n\nThis is robust and uses topological order based on actual file interfaces (input/output). Good.\n\nNow, final_report.py:\n\n```python\nimport hashlib, json\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\n\n\ndef _write(path, data):\n    p = Path(path)\n    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    p.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, indent=2, allow_nan=False) + '\\n', encoding='utf-8')\n\n\ndef main():\n    core = json.loads((ROOT / 'outputs/core_manifest.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\n    title = '数仓切换保障'\n    component_count = len(core['components'])\n    raw = json.dumps(core, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':'), allow_nan=False).encode('utf-8')\n    sha256 = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()\n    attention = []\n    for name, result in core['components'].items():\n        include = False\n        if isinstance(result, dict):\n            if result.get('blocked') is True:\n                include = True\n            for key in ('feasible', 'compatible'):\n                if key in result and result[key] is False:\n                    include = True\n            if 'deny' in result and result['deny'] > 0:\n                include = True\n            if 'score_total' in result and result['score_total'] >= 20:\n                include = True\n        if include and name not in attention:\n            attention.append(name)\n    attention.sort()\n    report = {\n        'title': title,\n        'component_count': component_count,\n        'sha256': sha256,\n        'attention': attention,\n    }\n    _write(ROOT / 'outputs/final_report.json', report)\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n```\n\nWait, the attention logic: \"blocked为真、feasible或compatible显式为false、deny>0、score_total>=20中任一条件成立即列入\". \n\n- \"blocked为真\" → result['blocked'] is True.\n- \"feasible或compatible显式为false\" → key in result and result[key] is False (explicit false). \"不存在的字段不触发，不能把空列表、空对象或数值0等同于显式false\". So only `is False` (not `== False`? `0 == False` is True in Python, so I must use `is False` to avoid treating 0 as false). 组件目录为`domains/backfill/`、`domains/freshness/`、`domains/checksums/`、`views/cutover_risk/`、`views/reviewer_load/`、`views/repair_waves/`。每个组件实现model.py、transform.py、render.py；根目录实现run_all.py、final_report.py。可添加必要的私有辅助模块。\n8: 不得修改README.md、任务说明、inputs/、任何contract.json、check_module.py、根check.py或integrate.py。已有受保护稳定接口保留。\n9: \n10: ## 公共函数接口与输入解释\n11: \n12: - `model.load_payload(path: Path)`：读取UTF-8 JSON，对象或数组顶层合法；非法JSON、顶层null/布尔/数字/字符串均抛ValueError。业务字段由transform检查。\n13: - `transform.transform(payloads: list, contract: dict)`：payloads严格按该契约inputs的顺序传入已读取的对象或数组，返回`{\"name\": contract[\"name\"], \"result\": 算法结果对象}`。输出恰有name/result两层；本说明各operation的输出字段均属于result，不得额外嵌套一次name/result。不得修改payloads或contract。\n14: - 输入对象仅当键集合恰好是`{\"name\",\"result\"}`时视为上游组件封装；先读取其result，再使用对应operation约定的字段。原始本地对象不是文件路径，也不当成records或nodes列表。多输入operation必须消费全部指定输入，不能复制固定上游结果。\n15: - `render.build_component(root: Path, contract_path: Path)`：读取该契约及其全部输入，调用transform并返回完整封装；执行组件render.py时写入contract.output。文件路径相对于工作区root；从任意当前目录启动均应正确，缺文件或子命令失败须失败。\n16: - int字段严格排除bool；有限数字允许int/float但排除bool、NaN、Infinity。非空文本先检查去首尾空白后不为空；是否返回去空白/小写形式由各operation明确规定，不全局改写身份字段。列表与字典按规定类型读取，不把null当空集合，不自动把数字字符串转换成数字。\n17: - 文中没有给出默认值的必要字段缺失、非法类型或明确列出的冲突抛ValueError；合法无解使用operation的无解结果，不等同于输入错误。词典序采用通常字符串/列表/元组顺序，相同前缀时较短列表更小。\n18: - contract中的name/operation/inputs/output是接口字段；只有下列业务规则显式使用的算法参数参与计算，不能看到threshold便对所有operation筛选。参数值读取本组件契约，不从样例推断固定常量。\n19: \n20: ### 证据公共字段\n21: \n22: 证据公共字段：每条输入均含id:非空str、revision:int>=0、owner:非空str、category:prepare/copy/verify、tags:list[非空str]、failures:int>=0、total:int>=1、lag:int>=0、valid:bool；failures<=total。所有整数排除bool。id去首尾空白；owner/category/tags去空白小写；tags去重排序。先校验全部记录与修订冲突，再取最新版本。只有本operation段落指定的公式决定score，其他operation的公式不适用。\n23: \n24: ## 各组件的完整业务规则\n25: \n26: ### domains/backfill — 修订证据失败率\n27: \n28: operation：`failure_evidence`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n29: \n30: 输入为记录数组。每项必须有 id:非空字符串、revision:整数>=0、owner:非空字符串、category:prepare/copy/verify、tags:字符串数组、failures:整数>=0、total:整数>=1、lag:整数>=0、valid:布尔；failures<=total。id去首尾空白；owner/category/tags去首尾空白并小写；tags去重排序。先校验每个输入版本，再按规范化id选最大revision。同id同revision的全部规范化字段必须相同，否则ValueError；相同副本去重，即使该版本不是最新版也检查冲突。评分仅为 ceil(20*failures/total)+offset，再截断到[0,20]。不使用lag、sla或valid计算本operation的评分。输出result={records:[{id,source,owner,tags,category,score}],count,score_total}；source=contract.name，records按(source,id)升序，count为最新记录数，score_total为这些记录分数之和。threshold不筛除证据记录。空输入records=[]，count=score_total=0。\n31: \n32: 局部检查命令：`python3 domains/backfill/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n33: \n34: ### domains/freshness — 修订证据延迟\n35: \n36: operation：`lag_evidence`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n37: \n38: 记录字段、规范化、全部修订的冲突检查、最新版选择和输出字段与本说明中的证据公共规则一致：id去空白；owner/category/tags去空白小写，tags去重排序；所有版本均校验，按id保留最大revision；相同id/revision规范化内容不一致报ValueError。每条记录仍必须包含failures、total、lag、valid等全部证据字段且满足类型/范围约束。评分仅为ceil(lag/sla)+offset，再截断到[0,20]，sla为契约中的正整数。不把失败率公式叠加到延迟公式上，不用threshold筛除证据记录。result={records:[{id,source,owner,tags,category,score}],count,score_total}，source=contract.name，records按(source,id)排序。空输入为空records及两个零统计。\n39: \n40: 局部检查命令：`python3 domains/freshness/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n41: \n42: ### domains/checksums — 修订证据完整性\n43: \n44: 记录字段、规范化、全部修订的冲突检查、最新版选择和输出字段与本说明中的证据公共规则一致：id去空白；owner/category/tags去空白小写，tags去重排序；所有版本均校验，按id保留最大revision；相同id/revision规范化内容不一致报ValueError。每条记录仍必须包含failures、total、lag、valid等全部证据字段且满足类型/范围约束。评分仅为(0 if valid else 15)+failures+offset，再截断到[0,20]；不使用ceil(20*failures/total)或延迟公式。不用threshold筛除证据记录。result={records:[{id,source,owner,tags,category,score}],count,score_total}，source=contract.name，records按(source,id)排序。空输入为空records及两个零统计。\n47: operation：`risk_ranking`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n53: \n54: 输入为按契约inputs顺序传入的组件封装；对每个封装读取result.records。以(source,id)合并：键相同且整条记录相同才可去重，否则ValueError。设合并集合为R，先选score>=threshold，再按(-score,source,id)排序，最后截取前limit项得到S。result.records、ranking、score_total全部基于同一个S，不能让records保留整个R。records仅改变展示顺序，按(source,id)升序；ranking按上述分数排名输出完整source+\":\"+id；score_total=sum(S.score)，不对R求和。输出result恰为{records,ranking,score_total}；S为空时分别为[]、[]、0。不得丢弃source前缀或再次按id跨source合并。\n55: \n56: 局部检查命令：`python3 views/cutover_risk/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n57: \n58: ### views/reviewer_load — 负载分配\n59: \n60: operation：`review_balance`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n61: \n62: 合并每个输入封装的result.records，以(source,id)去重，相同键内容冲突ValueError。使用全部合并记录，不再应用threshold、limit或其他筛选。按(-score,source,id)逐条处理，每次选当前累计score最小的reviewer，平局取reviewer名称字典序最小者；加入该记录score。reviewers来自本组件契约，不能只保留最终被分配到任务的人。result={records,assignments,loads}；records按(source,id)；assignments是{id:完整source+\":\"+id,reviewer}并按id排序；loads包含所有reviewer（包括零负载）。空输入仍返回完整零loads，其余两个列表为空。\n63: \n64: 局部检查命令：`python3 views/reviewer_load/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n65: \n66: ### views/repair_waves — 依赖波次与条件修复\n67: \n68: operation：`remediation_plan`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n69: \n70: 输入是组件封装，不是原始nodes数组。合并所有输入的result.records，包括审核分配组件返回的records；以(source,id)去重，相同键整条记录冲突ValueError。只读取records，不使用assignments、loads或feasible推导人员边、job/worker匹配或额外阻断。only_if_blocking=false时保留全部合并记录；为true时，若存在至少一条tags含blocking且score>=threshold的记录，也保留全部记录；否则使用空记录集合。此开关不是逐条筛选器。对保留集合，每条记录对应一个业务节点，完整id为source+\":\"+id，duration=1+score//5；category次序prepare<copy<verify，每个节点依赖集合中所有严格较早类别节点。按下述业务图规则计算waves/earliest_finish/critical_path/makespan：每波取本波开始时所有依赖已提交的节点并按id排序，整波结束后才取下一波；finish=duration+最大前驱finish，无前驱为duration；最大finish并列时，先比较前驱已经形成的完整路径列表（尚未追加当前节点）并取字典序最小，再追加当前节点；最终终点按finish最大、完整路径字典序最小选择。result={records,blocked:false,waves,critical_path,makespan,earliest_finish}；records按(source,id)，earliest_finish键按id。空集合时records/waves/critical_path=[]、makespan=0、earliest_finish={}、blocked仍为false。该业务图规则不表示Agent应怎样拆分或调度任务。\n71: \n72: 局部检查命令：`python3 views/repair_waves/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n73: \n74: ## JSON产物、集成与最终报告\n75: \n76: 所有写出的JSON文件采用UTF-8、ensure_ascii=False、sort_keys=True、indent=2、allow_nan=False，末尾加一个真实换行字符。紧凑摘要序列化与文件展示格式不同。\n77: `run_all.py`定位工作区，依据真实文件接口完成全部必要计算，执行已有集成与最终报告。不能用文件名排序代替执行条件，不能依赖旧outputs；各子进程失败传播非零退出码。已有`python3 integrate.py`读取组件产物生成`outputs/core_manifest.json`，其完整结构为`{\"components\": {组件名: 该组件的result}}`。\n78: 已有`python3 integrate.py domains`是一个数据转换接口：读取domains组件结果，按(source,id)合并records，冲突报错，写`outputs/domain_core.json`。只有现有契约实际引用该文件时才需要此产物；不要无条件增加接口中不存在的业务输入。\n79: \n80: `final_report.py`读取并解析完整`outputs/core_manifest.json`对象，写`outputs/final_report.json`，恰有title、component_count、sha256、attention四个字段。title准确为`数仓切换保障`；component_count为core_object[\"components\"]的键数。\n81: sha256的输入对象是完整core_object，包含外层components键；不是仅core_object[\"components\"]、不是原文件字节、不是多个文件摘要拼接。序列化准确为`json.dumps(core_object, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), allow_nan=False).encode(\"utf-8\")`，不加文件末尾换行，然后求SHA-256小写hex。\n82: attention是组件名去重排序列表：对每个result，blocked为真、feasible或compatible显式为false、deny>0、score_total>=20中任一条件成立即列入；不存在的字段不触发，不能把空列表、空对象或数值0等同于显式false。每个组件至多列一次。\n83: \n84: ## 验收与执行边界\n85: \n86: 在工作区运行`python3 证据公共字段\n\n证据公共字段：每条输入均含id:非空str、revision:int>=0、owner:非空str、category:prepare/copy/verify、tags:list[非空str]、failures:int>=0、total:int>=1、lag:int>=0、valid:bool；failures<=total。所有整数排除bool。id去首尾空白；owner/category/tags去空白小写；tags去重排序。先校验全部记录与修订冲突，再取最新版本。只有本operation段落指定的公式决定score，其他operation的公式不适用。\n\n## 各组件的完整业务规则\n\n### domains/backfill — 修订证据失败率\n\noperation：`failure_evidence`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入为记录数组。每项必须有 id:非空字符串、revision:整数>=0、owner:非空字符串、category:prepare/copy/verify、tags:字符串数组、failures:整数>=0、total:整数>=1、lag:整数>=0、valid:布尔；failures<=total。id去首尾空白；owner/category/tags去首尾空白并小写；tags去重排序。先校验每个输入版本，再按规范化id选最大revision。同id同revision的全部规范化字段必须相同，否则ValueError；相同副本去重，即使该版本不是最新版也检查冲突。评分仅为 ceil(20*failures/total)+offset，再截断到[0,20]。不使用lag、sla或valid计算本operation的评分。输出result={records:[{id,source,owner,tags,category,score}],count,score_total}；source=contract.name，records按(source,id)升序，count为最新记录数，score_total为这些记录分数之和。threshold不筛除证据记录。空输入records=[]，count=score_total=0。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 domains/backfill/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### domains/freshness — 修订证据延迟\n\noperation：`lag_evidence`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n记录字段、规范化、全部修订的冲突检查、最新版选择和输出字段与本说明中的证据公共规则一致：id去空白；owner/category/tags去空白小写，tags去重排序；所有版本均校验，按id保留最大revision；相同id/revision规范化内容不一致报ValueError。每条记录仍必须包含failures、total、lag、valid等全部证据字段且满足类型/范围约束。评分仅为ceil(lag/sla)+offset，再截断到[0,20]，sla为契约中的正整数。不把失败率公式叠加到延迟公式上，不用threshold筛除证据记录。result={records:[{id,source,owner,tags,category,score}],count,score_total}，source=contract.name，records按(source,id)排序。空输入为空records及两个零统计。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 domains/freshness/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### domains/checksums — 修订证据完整性\n\noperation：`integrity_evidence`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n记录字段、规范化、全部修订的冲突检查、最新版选择和输出字段与本说明中的证据公共规则一致：id去空白；owner/category/tags去空白小写，tags去重排序；所有版本均校验，按id保留最大revision；相同id/revision规范化内容不一致报ValueError。每条记录仍必须包含failures、total、lag、valid等全部证据字段且满足类型/范围约束。评分仅为(0 if valid else 15)+failures+offset，再截断到[0,20]；不使用ceil(20*failures/total)或延迟公式。不用threshold筛除证据记录。result={records:[{id,source,owner,tags,category,score}],count,score_total}，source=contract.name，records按(source,id)排序。空输入为空records及两个零统计。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 风险排名\n\noperation：`risk_ranking`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入为按契约inputs顺序传入的组件封装；对每个封装读取result.records。以(source,id)合并：键相同且整条记录相同才可去重，否则ValueError。设合并集合为R，先选score>=threshold，再按(-score,source,id)排序，最后截取前limit项得到S。result.records、ranking、score_total全部基于同一个S，不能让records保留整个R。records仅改变展示顺序，按(source,id)升序；ranking按上述分数排名输出完整source+\":\"+id；score_total=sum(S.score)，不对R求和。输出result恰为{records,ranking,score_total}；S为空时分别为[]、[]、0。不得丢弃source前缀或再次按id跨source合并。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 依赖波次与条件修复\n\noperation：`remediation_plan`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入是组件封装，不是原始nodes数组。合并所有输入的result.records，包括审核分配组件返回的records；以(source,id)去重，相同键整条记录冲突ValueError。只读取records，不使用assignments、loads或feasible推导人员边、job/worker匹配或额外阻断。only_if_blocking=false时保留全部合并记录；为true时，若存在至少一条tags含blocking且score>=threshold的记录，也保留全部记录；否则使用空记录集合。此开关不是逐条筛选器。对保留集合，每条记录对应一个业务节点，完整id为source+\":\"+id，duration=1+score//5；category次序prepare<copy<verify，每个节点依赖集合中所有严格较早类别节点。按下述业务图规则计算waves/earliest_finish/critical_path/makespan：每波取本波开始时所有依赖已提交的节点并按id排序，整波结束后才取下一波；finish=duration+最大前驱finish，无前驱为duration；最大finish并列时，先比较前驱已经形成的完整路径列表（尚未追加当前节点）并取字典序最小，再追加当前节点；最终终点按finish最大、完整路径字典序最小选择。result={records,blocked:false,waves,critical_path,makespan,earliest_finish}；records按(source,id)，earliest_finish键按id。空集合时records/waves/critical_path=[]、makespan=0、earliest_finish={}、blocked仍为false。该业务图规则不表示Agent应怎样拆分或调度任务。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 views/repair_waves/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n## The core logic:\n\nEvidence normalization function:\n- validate each record has all fields: id (non-empty str after strip), revision (int>=0, not bool), owner (non-empty str), category (prepare/copy/verify after strip+lower), tags (list of non-empty str), failures (int>=0 not bool), total (int>=1 not bool), lag (int>=0 not bool), valid (bool), failures<=total.\n\nWait: \"所有整数排除bool\" — all integers exclude bool. Identical copies (same normalized content) dedup. Conflicts → ValueError.\n\nThen pick max revision per id.\n\nThen score per operation.\n\nNow, what fields are \"fully normalized fields\" for conflict checking? The normalized record includes id, revision, owner, category, tags, failures, total, lag, valid. Actually for conflict checking: \"同id同revision的全部规范化字段必须相同\" - same id+revision must have all normalized fields identical. But for conflict checking we compare the normalized evidence fields. Let me think about which fields: id (normalized), revision, owner, category, tags, failures, total, lag, valid. Since id and revision are the key, \"all normalized fields\" refers to owner, category, tags, failures, total, lag, valid (all normalized). Two records with same (id, revision) must have identical normalized values for all these fields, else ValueError.\n\nLet me define normalization producing a canonical record dict with fields: id, revision, owner, category, tags, failures, total, lag, valid.\n\nFor conflict detection: group by (id, revision). Within group, compare the full normalized record (excluding... well id and revision are same by grouping). If two records differ in any field → ValueError. If identical → dedup (keep one).\n\nWait, \"相同副本去重，即使该版本不是最新版也检查冲突\" — identical copies dedup, and even if that version is not the latest, still check conflict. So conflict check applies to ALL revisions, not just latest. Good — we group by (id, revision) across all records.\n\nSo algorithm:\n1. Parse all records, normalize each, validate. Collect list of normalized records.\n2. Group by (id, revision). For each group, all records must be identical (full normalized equality). If any two differ → ValueError.\n3. Dedup groups to one representative per (id, revision).\n4. For each id, select the record with max revision.\n5. Compute score.\n6. Build output records with fields {id, source, owner, tags, category, score}, sorted by (source, id). source = contract[\"name\"].\n\nNow scoring formulas:\n\nbackfill failure_evidence: score = clamp(ceil(20*failures/total) + offset, 0, 20), offset=-1.\n\nUse math.ceil. 20*failures/total is a float. ceil then + offset then clamp to [0,20].\n\nfreshness lag_evidence: score = clamp(ceil(lag/sla) + offset, 0, 20), offset=0, sla=contract[\"sla\"] positive int.\n\nchecksums integrity_evidence: score = clamp((0 if valid else 15) + failures + offset, 0, 20), offset=1.\n\nNote these formulas use int/float. Let me make clamp return int.\n\nNow, \"int字段严格排除bool\" — score should be int. clamp to [0,20] integer.\n\nNow, the transform signature: `transform(payloads: list, contract: dict)`. payloads are already loaded objects (list of lists for domain inputs since top-level arrays). I'll use a shared module.\n\nLet me design `/app/common.py` containing:\n\n```python\nimport json, math\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nCATEGORIES = ('prepare', 'copy', 'verify')\n\ndef load_payload(path):\n    ...\n\ndef _is_int(x): return isinstance(x, int) and not isinstance(x, bool)\n\ndef _norm_str(v):\n    if not isinstance(v, str): raise ValueError(...)\n    s = v.strip()\n    if not s: raise ValueError(...)\n    return s\n\ndef _norm_text_lower(v):\n    # strip + lower, must be non-empty after strip\n    if not isinstance(v, str): raise ValueError\n    s = v.strip()\n    if not s: raise ValueError\n    return s.lower()\n\ndef parse_evidence(records):\n    # records is a list\n    if not isinstance(records, list): raise ValueError\n    normalized = []\n    for rec in records:\n        normalized.append(normalize_record(rec))\n    return normalized\n\ndef normalize_record(rec):\n    if not isinstance(rec, dict): raise ValueError\n    # required fields\n    for f in ('id','revision','owner','category','tags','failures','total','lag','valid'):\n        if f not in rec: raise ValueError\n    rid = _norm_str(rec['id'])\n    revision = rec['revision']\n    if not _is_int(revision) or revision < 0: raise ValueError\n    owner = _norm_text_lower(rec['owner'])\n    category = _norm_text_lower(rec['category'])\n    if category not in CATEGORIES: raise ValueError\n    tags = rec['tags']\n    if not isinstance(tags, list): raise ValueError\n    norm_tags = []\n    for t in tags:\n        nt = _norm_text_lower(t)\n        norm_tags.append(nt)\n    norm_tags = sorted(set(norm_tags))\n    failures = rec['failures']\n    if not _is_int(failures) or failures < 0: raise ValueError\n    total = rec['total']\n    if not _is_int(total) or total < 1: raise ValueError\n    lag = rec['lag']\n    if not _is_int(lag) or lag < 0: raise ValueError\n    valid = rec['valid']\n    if not isinstance(valid, bool): raise ValueError\n    if failures > total: raise ValueError\n    return {'id': rid, 'revision': revision, 'owner': owner, 'category': category, 'tags': norm_tags, 'failures': failures, 'total': total, 'lag': lag, 'valid': valid}\n```\n\nWait, should I check for extra fields? Good.\n\nNow for conflict/dedup:\n\n```python\ndef dedup_latest(records):\n    groups = {}\n    for rec in records:\n        key = (rec['id'], rec['revision'])\n        groups.setdefault(key, []).append(rec)\n    # conflict check\n    latest = {}\n    for key, recs in groups.items():\n        first = recs[0]\n        for r in recs[1:]:\n            if r != first: raise ValueError(...)\n        rid, rev = key\n        if rid not in latest or rev > latest[rid]['revision']:\n            latest[rid] = first\n    return list(latest.values())\n```\n\nWait but the conflict check should compare full normalized record. Good.\n\nNow scoring:\n\n```python\ndef clamp(v, lo=0, hi=20):\n    return max(lo, min(hi, v))\n\ndef score_failure(rec, offset):\n    return clamp(math.ceil(20 * rec['failures'] / rec['total']) + offset)\n\ndef score_lag(rec, offset, sla):\n    return clamp(math.ceil(rec['lag'] / sla) + offset)\n\ndef score_integrity(rec, offset):\n    return clamp((0 if rec['valid'] else 15) + rec['failures'] + offset)\n```\n\nNote: offset is an int from contract. math.ceil returns int. Good.\n\nNow let me write all the files.\n\nLet me first write the shared module.\n\nActually, I realize I should double-check one thing about `math.ceil` and the score for backfill: `20 * failures / total`. In Python, `20 * failures / total` where both ints → float division. `math.ceil` returns int. Good. But to be safe with exact semantics, use `math.ceil(20 * failures / total)`. Good.\n\nNow, let me write everything.\n\nLet me structure the shared module:\n\n```python\nimport json\nimport math\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nCATEGORIES = (\"prepare\", \"copy\", \"verify\")\n\n\ndef load_payload(path):\n    try:\n        data = json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:\n        raise ValueError(\"invalid JSON: %s\" % exc) from exc\n    if not isinstance(data, (dict, list)):\n        raise ValueError(\"top-level must be object or array\")\n    return data\n\n\ndef _is_int(value):\n    return isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool)\n\n\ndef _norm_text(value, lower):\n    if not isinstance(value, str):\n        raise ValueError(\"expected string\")\n    s = value.strip()\n    if not s:\n        raise ValueError(\"empty string\")\n    return s.lower() if lower else s\n\n\ndef _normalize_record(rec):\n    if not isinstance(rec, dict):\n        raise ValueError(\"record must be object\")\n    for field in (\"id\", \"revision\", \"owner\", \"category\", \"tags\", \"failures\", \"total\", \"lag\", \"valid\"):\n        if field not in rec:\n            raise ValueError(\"missing field: %s\" % field)\n    rid = _norm_text(rec[\"id\"], False)\n    revision = rec[\"revision\"]\n    if not _is_int(revision) or revision < 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"bad revision\")\n    owner = _norm_text(rec[\"owner\"], True)\n    category = _norm_text(rec[\"category\"], True)\n    if category not in CATEGORIES:\n        raise ValueError(\"bad category\")\n    tags = rec[\"tags\"]\n    if not isinstance(tags, list):\n        raise ValueError(\"tags must be list\")\n    norm_tags = sorted({_norm_text(t, True) for t in tags})\n    failures = rec[\"failures\"]\n    if not _is_int(failures) or failures < 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"bad failures\")\n    total = rec[\"total\"]\n    if not _is_int(total) or total < 1:\n        raise ValueError(\"bad total\")\n    lag = rec[\"lag\"]\n    if not _is_int(lag) or lag < 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"bad lag\")\n    valid = rec[\"valid\"]\n    if not isinstance(valid, bool):\n        raise ValueError(\"bad valid\")\n    if failures > total:\n        raise ValueError(\"failures > total\")\n    return {\n        \"id\": rid,\n        \"revision\": revision,\n        \"owner\": owner,\n        \"category\": category,\n        \"tags\": norm_tags,\n        \"failures\": failures,\n        \"total\": total,\n        \"lag\": lag,\n        \"valid\": valid,\n    }\n\n\ndef parse_evidence(records):\n    if not isinstance(records, list):\n        raise ValueError(\"input must be array\")\n    return [_normalize_record(r) for r in records]\n\n\ndef select_latest(records):\n    groups = {}\n    for rec in records:\n        groups.setdefault((rec[\"id\"], rec[\"revision\"]), []).append(rec)\n    latest = {}\n    for (rid, revision), recs in groups.items():\n        first = recs[0]\n        for r in recs[1:]:\n            if r != first:\n                raise ValueError(\"revision conflict for %s r%s\" % (rid, revision))\n        if rid not in latest or revision > latest[rid][\"revision\"]:\n            latest[rid] = first\n    return list(latest.values())\n\n\ndef clamp_score(value):\n    return max(0, min(20, value))\n\n\ndef score_failure(rec, offset):\n    return clamp_score(math.ceil(20 * rec[\"failures\"] / rec[\"total\"]) + offset)\n\n\ndef score_lag(rec, offset, sla):\n    return clamp_score(math.ceil(rec[\"lag\"] / sla) + offset)\n\n\ndef score_integrity(rec, offset):\n    return clamp_score((0 if rec[\"valid\"] else 15) + rec[\"failures\"] + offset)\n\n\ndef is_wrapper(value):\n    return isinstance(value, dict) and set(value) == {\"name\", \"result\"}\n\n\ndef unwrap_records(payload):\n    if not is_wrapper(payload):\n        raise ValueError(\"expected wrapper {name,result}\")\n    result = payload[\"result\"]\n    if not isinstance(result, dict):\n        raise ValueError(\"wrapper result must be object\")\n    records = result.get(\"records\")\n    if not isinstance(records, list):\n        raise ValueError(\"wrapper result.records must be list\")\n    return records\n\n\ndef merge_records(record_lists):\n    merged = {}\n    for records in record_lists:\n        for r in records:\n            if not isinstance(r, dict) or \"source\" not in r or \"id\" not in r:\n                raise ValueError(\"bad record\")\n            key = (r[\"source\"], r[\"id\"])\n            if key in merged and merged[key] != r:\n                raise ValueError(\"record conflict for %s:%s\" % key)\n            merged[key] = r\n    return list(merged.values())\n```\n\nNow, model.py for each
